# Hand fully covered countries back to EU4 elections when the list ends.
country_event = {
	id = eu4_2k_politics.900
	title = eu4_2k_politics.900.t
	desc = eu4_2k_politics.900.d
	picture = ELECTION_REPUBLICAN_eventPicture
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = eu4_2k_politics.900.a
		if = { limit = { has_reform = eu4_2k_scripted_leadership } remove_government_reform = eu4_2k_scripted_leadership }
	}
}
